If invalid, stop. Take the smallest safe action. Run the cheapest target check. If it passes, stop. If it fails, diagnose once and recover once. Repeat failure or no new evidence: stop and report. func : callable\n            Function which can be called in the form `func(x, axis, **kwargs)`\n            to return the result of collapsing an np.ndarray over the coarsening\n            dimensions.
